# RC speed-up trial: conservative timing and wait census

Source: `ec893877a59a3490bc2aa83c8e6ebd3303ecd910`, inspected 2026-10-29 for round-2 Plan task `acbb52cb`. This is a source census, not execution evidence. The parent [plan](../superpowers/plans/2026-10-29-card-1514-rc-full-speedups-plan.md) owns admission and the Windows-only trial.

Every method and argument expansion in each listed test file inherits its disposition, including tests whose wait is in a shared helper or partial class. Conservatively excluding the whole file avoids pretending that lexical matches identify a complete call graph. No unseen/uncatalogued test can enter the diagnostic process: the driver admits only the two exact candidate methods and checks the expanded UID multiset. All other assemblies, git-lane tests, native ownership controls, elapsed benchmarks and script harnesses are excluded from concurrent scheduling on the trial host. Ordinary Full coverage is unchanged.

The scan includes all tracked C# under tests and test-*.ps1 under scripts, looking for `Elapsed`, `Stopwatch`, wall/CPU median or overhead bounds, real delay/sleep, wait/poll APIs, cancellation deadlines, UTC/system clocks and Timeout attributes. It additionally includes all strict-limiter files even without a match. The round-3 supplement also scans tracked C# for `ParallelLimiter<GitProcessSpawnLimit>` and includes the reviewed `Start-Sleep`/shell-sleep strings in WorkspaceHookRunnerTests (60 s), LandProtocolCrashWorker (180 s), and LandVerificationWindowHarness (20 ms polling until its release file). Source strings execute in child processes and are real waits. These three files are excluded in full, including their consumers outside the closed candidate closure. False positives such as virtual-clock assertions and fixture source strings are intentionally retained. This is a conservative scheduling census, not a claim that every hit uses real time. Source or helper changes require a fresh census and closure review before any roster expansion.

The only admitted candidate closure is C1514LandingSplitBudgetTests -> ScriptHarness.RunHarnessCaseAsync -> ScriptHarnessProcess/Options -> test-nightly-tests.ps1 exact C1404/C1410 case -> nightly planner/coverage helpers. Each child owns a GUID ResultsDirectory. Retain the existing cancellation/cleanup deadlines and prevent shared policy-file writes; the tests read frozen costs/policy. All fixtures remain invocation-local. Code must retain this closure in the diagnostic manifest and reject drift. The two cases test synthetic planning; their speed result cannot represent DB, ConPTY, telemetry or the 30 s wait band.

Round-2 inventory (historical): 478 strict-limiter files; 1539 files with the original timing/wait markers; 1616 listed paths. Round-3 supplement at `6e3282ea12f8eb4386caaca060c03e52a2622a69`: **1653 distinct paths**, adding the three embedded real-sleep/poll files and 34 previously unlisted GitProcessSpawnLimit files. All **111** tracked C# files matching `ParallelLimiter<GitProcessSpawnLimit>` (including classification-test fixture text) are now listed; 77 were already present through timing markers. This does not add any candidate UID or change any limiter. The original marker count is retained as historical, not claimed to be a rerun of its heuristic. Line numbers refer to the frozen source above.
